…RESPONDENTS
(By Smt. Niloufer Akbar, AGA for R1, R3 & R4;
Sri G.V. Shashikumar, Advocate for R2)
THIS WRIT PETITION IS FILED UNDER ARTICLES
226 AND 227 OF THE CONSTITUTION OF INDIA,
PRAYING TO DECLARE THAT SECTION 20(2)(a-iv)
AND (a-v) OF THE KARNATAKA CO-OPERATIVE
SOCIETIES ACT, 1959 ARE ULTRA VIRES OF THE
CONSTITUTION OF INDIA, ETC.
 THIS PETITION COMING ON FOR PRELIMINARY
HEARING THIS DAY, THE COURT MADE THE
FOLLOWING:-
ORDER
Heard Sri Ravindra Prasad B., learned Advocate
for the petitioners, Smt. Niloufer Akbar, learned AGA
for the State and Shri G.V.Shashi Kumar, learned
Advocate for respondent No.2.
2. Sri Ravindra Prasad B., learned Advocate for the
petitioners submits that elections have already been
conducted. Therefore, this petition is rendered
infructuous and accordingly prayed for its dismissal.

407
He undertakes to file a memo to this effect.
Once the memo is received, the Registry shall keep
the same in the record.
3. In the circumstances, the petition is accordingly
dismissed as having become infructuous.
No costs.
 Sd/-
 JUDGE
